# Skills · D · part 124

810 entries, diagram to diagram-to-infra. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **diagram**: Creates Excalidraw diagram files (.excalidraw) that make a visual argument — workflows, solution architectures, deal and stakeholder flows, integration landscapes, concepts. Use on "diagram this", "architecture diagram", "draw a flow", "make a diagram of", "visualize this process". Siblings: toc-bbit-expert (draws its own Cloud/CRT/FRT diagrams), pptx-generator (slides). SKIP for data charts and plots (bar, line, pie, dashboards) — that is data visualisation, not Excalidraw. ([hangljoe/AI-PreSales-Skills](https://github.com/hangljoe/AI-PreSales-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/diagram) · ★5)
